## DYNASTY  (15.)
MODE: RESEARCH -- Gen 1 Animal, the fold, Gen 3 Angel, the heir: not started on any surface and never owned. Research only; findings become PEOPLE, QUESTS and RUN jobs when those open. Canon (who marries whom, who the Angel is) stays his; the lane brings shapes.
STATE: Gen 1 Animal, the fold, Gen 3 Angel: nothing on any player surface; selectHeir exists with zero callers; nothing ever writes a child. This lane does not implement; findings become PEOPLE, RUN and (when reopened) QUESTS jobs. Canon stays his. MEASURED 9/5 by Q1 and worse than the line above: THE FOLD IS GENERATION-BLIND -- `gen` is compared twice in the whole fold block and both are bookkeeping, so gen 1 folds the same standings, territory, builds and economyCapacity as gen 2, and an animal has none of those, so running gen 1 through the fold as written hands gen 2 a block of zeroes, which is a RUN. selectHeir also cannot do the animal-to-human handoff at all: it picks a child, else a sibling's child, else null. THE WORD ANIMAL APPEARS IN THE DYNASTY ENGINE EXACTLY ONCE AND IT IS IN A COMMENT. There is no smell mechanic anywhere (olfact 0) and no four-legged renderer anywhere. The wildlife module is excellent and the coyote is its only reacts:false row. MEASURED 9/5 by Q2: WE SHIPPED BOTH HALVES OF THE COYOTE'S LIFE AND NEVER NOTICED THEY WERE THE SAME ANIMAL -- the wildlife module's lone dawn coyote (flock [1,1], reacts:false) is a TRANSIENT and the packs module's spaced family with a den (size [2,6], spacing 140, notice 40) is a RESIDENT, which is the real ethological split (measured mean range ~9 km2 held versus ~59 km2 homeless), and nothing in the game says the lone one is trying to become the group. A territory is never inherited, it is waited for; the pair bond ends only at death (236 coyotes, six years, zero cheating); and the number one killer of an urban coyote is a car, which our Vegas does not have, so gen 1's danger is people. AND Q1's claim that no four-legged renderer exists was FALSE and is corrected in place: draw_beast makes a 16x16 three-frame coyote shared with the dogs. What is missing is a coyote at the PLAYER's scale with facings. MEASURED 9/5 by Q3 (RESTORED, my own rebase resolver dropped it once): THE FOLD HAS THE PERSISTENCE BACKWARDS. It folds eight kinds of thing and decays exactly ONE -- a faction standing loses 25% a generation while territory, builds, economyCapacity, invest, karma and virtues carry at 100% forever -- and the measured world is the reverse: social status persists at about 0.79 a generation (ten to fifteen generations to fade) while wealth and occupation persist at about 0.3 to 0.4. AND THE RATE IS RIGHT ON THE WRONG FIELD: STANDING_DECAY_TO_NEUTRAL multiplies by 0.75 against a measured 0.79, a constant its own comment calls tunable that nobody ever checked. The fold also carries FOUR asset ledgers and not one object, name or memory: a relative is {id, rel, alive} with NO NAME and a wound is a bare target with no who and no when. MEASURED 9/5 by Q4: OUR THIRD GENERATION CANNOT GET WORSE. builds is a hard ratchet (Math.max), invest/economyCapacity/karma/virtues only accumulate, blindSpot and recordedKnown only grow, and THERE IS NO -= AND NO Math.min ANYWHERE IN THE FOLD, so districtTexture (invest<=0 apocalypse, <5 recovering, else modern) only ever travels one way and act 3's city cannot look worse than act 2's. AND THE TWO THINGS THE RESEARCH SAYS MAKE A THIRD GENERATION HARD ARE THE TWO WE CANNOT REPRESENT: dilution (selectHeir names ONE child and the other children are never referenced again anywhere, so there are no cousins, branches or shares) and forgetting (wounds appears EXACTLY TWICE in the engine, the declaration and one push, so nothing ever reads a wound and nothing ever settles one). THE ROW'S OWN PREMISE IS FOLKLORE: the 30/12/3 and 70% survival figures are contested and traced to a study about family communication, so DO NOT BUILD A CURSE. MEASURED 9/5 by Q5: NOBODY IN BOHEMIA HAS AN AGE. A person's age takes EXACTLY TWO VALUES in the whole engine, age:'child' and age:'adult' in story_surface, only to pick a cutscene sprite (control: age:'industrial' is a building style; the fight's 39 age/old hits are all hold/gold/bold/damage/wager plus two elapsed-time variables and one game title in a comment, so there is NO age in the fight at all). Everywhere else `age` in this engine means ELAPSED TIME, not how old somebody is. AND WE ALREADY BUILT THE AGEING EQUATION AND POINTED IT AT MEMORIES: bohemia_memory.js runs clarity = 0.5^(age/halflife) with halflife growing as log2 of familiarity, commented 'familiarity slows the fog', which is exactly the measured shape of a person ageing (raw speed decays, experience extends the halflife with diminishing returns). Third time this lane has found the right mechanism attached to the wrong subject. The real finding is that ageing is a CROSSOVER not a slope: reaction time is 20-30% slower by sixty while anticipation and pattern recognition RESIST age, so the number that falls is speed and the number that rises is knowing what happens next; healing is the one cost with no compensation. NOTE the PEOPLE lane already refused birth years and a locked calendar year on purpose, and this round agrees: any ageing must work on beats, not dates. MEASURED 9/5 by Q6: WE HAVE TWO MEMORY SYSTEMS AND THEY HAVE NEVER MET. bohemia_engine.js (the fold) references bohemia_standing ZERO times, so at the generational cut the fold decays an abstract faction number by 25% while the WITNESS WEB -- the organ that actually models a deed being seen, told, distorted and forgotten (clarity=0.5^(age/halflife)) -- is not advanced at all, *** CORRECTED BY Q7: that half was a FALSE NEGATIVE and it was wrong when I published it. The walked city's own ctFold() DOES call BohemiaStanding.inherit() and carries a parent's retold deeds forward, marked `inherited`, behind a door he controls (FACTIONS commit 383219e, already on main before my round-6 push). I had grepped engine/*.js for two function names I INVENTED and never grepped the slices for the real one. WHAT SURVIVES IS SHARPER: there are TWO FOLDS IN TWO FILES doing two halves -- the engine's foldGeneration carries the LEDGER, the city's ctFold carries the MEMORY -- and neither knows the other exists. The row is not 'build the wire', it is 'there are two folds and there should be one'. *** MEASURED 9/5 by Q7: A CHILD IS NOT A THING YOU CARRY, IT IS A REASON OTHER PEOPLE COME TO YOUR HOUSE. The genre's answer to a dependent is the escort mission, the most reliably hated mechanic in the medium; the species answer is that humans are COOPERATIVE BREEDERS and a child is raised by a group, so raising one alone is not the realistic option, it is the LEAST realistic one. That turns the worst mechanic in games into a query against the standing web, which already answers who would speak for you with a name. AND THE PARTNER HALF WAS ALREADY ANSWERED INSIDE A MODULE ABOUT FACTION STANDING: bohemia_resolve.js carries RATION (limit by count, never price), a CEILING that only moves on a COMMITMENT so you cannot grind past a relationship, and neglect that costs MORE the closer you are, which is the whole answer to 'without a chore' -- the cost of a partner is not maintenance, it is that ignoring them hurts more the more they matter. MEASURED: the fight's ally is ROSA (draft), ARCH.human, same 60 hp as every goon, leash 6, ALLY_DOWN_TURNS 99 with its own comment 'picking him up is not built yet and is not pretended'. NO CHILD EXISTS AS A PERSON IN THE ENGINE: the only children are a corpse described as child-sized, rig child-nodes, the fold's child event id, and the deeds comment 'A NOTORIOUS ONE BECOMES THE THING YOUR CHILD IS JUDGED FOR'. (Scope stated after round 6's false negative: engine searched, slices not swept.) MEASURED 9/5 by Q8: SPOKEN MEMORY LASTS 80 TO 110 YEARS AND THREE TO FOUR GENERATIONS, then the FLOATING GAP, after which only what was written, ritualised or built survives. BOHEMIA IS A HUNDRED YEARS AND THREE GENERATIONS, so the game is exactly as long as a family's spoken memory. WE BUILT THE FADING HALF BEAUTIFULLY: the standing web has SEE_RANGE 9, MAX_HOPS 2, HEARSAY_LOSS 0.55, GOSSIP_WINDOW 45, GEN_LOSS 0.45, and inherit() keeps only deeds with hops>0 ('the eyewitness is dead. Only what was RETOLD is still in the valley'), returning {carried, died}; legendOf() reports per deed how many still tell it, its force, and how many generations it survived. AND THE HALF THAT LASTS FOREVER IS AN IF-ELSE: monumentForm takes the FINAL fold's karma plus a summed sacrifice virtue and returns one of THREE strings for a hundred years of play, unable to see a single deed or name while legendOf one repo away knows exactly which survived. WE MODELLED THE TEMPORARY THING IN FINE GRAIN AND THE PERMANENT THING AS A COIN FLIP. Real forgetting is fast: 53% cannot name four grandparents, 21% cannot name one great-grandparent, and 84% say heritage matters, which is the feeling the heir should have. MEASURED 9/5 by Q9: THE CODE CANNOT OBEY HALF OF HIS OWN LOCKED LAW. The CENTURY RULE (7/26) says 'Neglect production/power/clout and act three's city is visibly POORER; invest and it's visibly rebuilt' -- but invest only accumulates and districtTexture (the ONLY reader of invest anywhere) only climbs apocalypse to recovering to modern, so 'visibly rebuilt' is buildable today and 'visibly poorer' is ARITHMETICALLY IMPOSSIBLE. We built the half of the century rule that rewards and not the half that costs. AND THE PATTERN FROM Q8 CONFIRMS IN A SECOND PLACE: the monument is three strings, a district is three textures, so EVERY PERMANENT THING IN THIS GAME IS THREE-VALUED while the things that fade are modelled in fine grain. THE REAL SHAPE: a town changes at THREE SPEEDS -- the street plan is most persistent (and ours is seed-fixed, the right answer for the wrong reason), plots outlive walls, fabric turns over in decades, and WHAT A PLACE IS FOR changes fastest and is not modelled at all. Renaming is rarer than anyone guesses: after a total regime collapse, five studied cities renamed 6.6% to 26% of streets, usually under a tenth, and only the politicised and central ones, so NINE NAMES IN TEN SURVIVE and what is forgotten is the REASON, not the name. AND THE REMNANTS ALREADY SAY 'Somebody has to remember what the street names were' and 'We kept the records' -- a cultural-memory faction shipped into a game with no street names. MEASURED 9/5 by Q10: THE SHAPE OF ACT 3 IS ALREADY BUILT AND WORKS ON HIS PHONE. The walked city carries SKY_BANDS=[[0.25,'REGION'],[0.55,'PLANET'],[1.01,'MOON']] on ONE scalar 0 to 1, same iso projection at every band so the diamond never breaks, debugged on his actual phone 8/13. What I did not find is anything the Angel can DO up there (scope: engine/*.js and slices/*.html searched). THE FINDING: the instinct for a transcendent act is NEW POWERS, and that is the one move that would break it. Far transfer between loosely related domains is very hard to document, expertise is domain-specific, and the longer you train a skill the MORE specific it gets, so mastery LIMITS transfer: a new rule set in the last act makes the player a beginner at the exact moment the story says they became a god. HIS OWN ZOOM LAW ALREADY HOLDS THE ANSWER: one camera, one scalar, same diamond, no dead ends. THE RULES MUST NOT CHANGE, THE SCALE CHANGES -- near transfer, which works. AND THE COST IS ALREADY ON THE SCREEN: experiments on power found high-power people draw an E on their forehead facing themselves and read others' emotions less accurately, so POWER COSTS PERSPECTIVE, and at planetary zoom a person is not visible. THE ANGEL'S POWER AND THE ANGEL'S COST ARE THE SAME GESTURE. Angel appears 53 times in the laws and TWICE in the engine. MEASURED 9/5 by Q11: THE MOUNTAIN SCARS, THE DISTRICT SCARS, THE PERSON DOES NOT. The only scars in the engine are a 'rockfall scar' on a cliff face in bohemia_mountain.js and the words 'combat scars' describing a DISTRICT's standing pressures (control: 18 'scar' hits and most are 'scarcity'). We gave the geology a memory of injury and not the family. SAY THE GOOD PART: a death already reorganises the world. bohemia_succession.js is 271 lines, IS CALLED (mandate module and walked city), holds roles as requirements not people, makes a vacancy a CONTESTED event with a winner, resolves on a fuse, and closes with a consequence ripple rather than an error if nobody can fill it. That is the craft's own answer, built. BUT THE CLOCK IS THREE ORDERS OF MAGNITUDE SHORT: fuseFor returns DAYS (2 uncontested, 17 with five claimants) while the locked succession law says consequences 'bloom in decade 2 and 3'. Same class of gap as Q9's century rule: good mechanism, clock too short for the story. AND A DEATH IN THE FOLD IS ONE BOOLEAN (alive=false). THE BIOLOGY: a scar is not damage, it is a repair that never finishes -- never full strength again, never regrows hair, oil or sweat glands, collagen in parallel bundles instead of a basket weave. A STATE, NOT A SUBTRACTION, which is how a permanent injury lives inside NO DAMAGE BEFORE THE DIAL. The real fraction is deliberately NOT shipped; the shape is ours and the value is his. MEASURED 9/5 by Q12: THE MACHINE ALREADY COUNTS EXACTLY WHAT THE FIRST HOUR NEEDS AND TELLS NOBODY. ctFold() returns {gen, carried, died} -- how many of your father's deeds survived because somebody RETOLD them, and how many died with the last witness -- and `carried`/`died` appear in EXACTLY TWO PLACES in the walked city, where they are computed and where they are returned. Nothing consumes them, nothing displays them. AND THE ORDER IS THE WHOLE DESIGN: losses are felt harder than equal gains, so `died` shown first is the loss quantified at the moment it lands hardest, while `carried` is the only thing that answers the question. WHAT YOU KEPT COMES FIRST. Also: the heir keeps the player id '@' by ruling, so every card, rung and outfit view still works -- MECHANICALLY THE PLAYER LOSES NOTHING AND WOULD STILL FEEL THEY LOST EVERYTHING, because nothing tells them what they kept. And a scene is ONE FILE: four act-1 scenes shipped (115 to 362 lines, 'it cost one file, no new engine code') and THERE IS NO FOLD SCENE. The loss-aversion coefficient is deliberately NOT shipped: the direction is well supported and the number is contested in its own literature. MEASURED 9/5 by the new Q11 [what carries]: DEBT DOES NOT INHERIT. A child is not personally liable for a parent's unsecured debts; creditors claim against the ESTATE and an insolvent estate means the creditor loses. The exceptions prove it (co-signing was always your own debt; community property reaches a SPOUSE; filial responsibility laws exist in about two dozen US states and are rarely enforced). YOU DO NOT INHERIT A BILL, YOU INHERIT LESS AND YOU INHERIT THE PEOPLE HE OWED. AND THAT IS WHERE IT BECOMES OURS: DEBT STOPS INHERITING ONLY BECAUSE THERE IS A COURT TO STOP IT, AND BOHEMIA HAS NO COURTS, so the standing web's 'born owing what your father owed' is not a fantasy mechanic, it is what the law exists to prevent running where the law is gone. THE TRADE CARRIES AS A PULL, NOT A DESTINY: a son of a military father is 5x more likely to enlist and only 1 in 4 does; only 3% of farmers' sons stay and that 3% is 7.6x the base rate. Huge multipliers, small absolutes. THE ROW'S DELIVERABLE, THE FULL FIELD-BY-FIELD CARRY LIST (good and bad, plus WHAT MUST DIE: the bill, his hands, the witnesses, the reason for a name, the cast), is section 4 of the record and it is the ruling input Q3's fix was waiting for. MEASURED 9/5 by the new Q12 [heir plays]: THE DIFFERENCE CANNOT LIVE IN WHAT THE HEIR CAN DO (round 10 rules out new verbs, round 13 rules out carried skill), and the research puts it somewhere else entirely: the best-studied second generation (children of immigrants) was expected to DECLINE and showed broad upward mobility instead, and the named mechanism is a POSITION rather than a trait -- THEY CAN SELECT FROM BOTH WORLDS, knowing what the parent knew without being bound by it. That is an ADVANTAGE, and it is the leg up an heir must arrive with, at no stat cost. THE HEIR IS NOT A NEW CHARACTER, THE HEIR IS THE SAME HANDS IN A ROOM THAT HAS ALREADY DECIDED SOMETHING ABOUT THEM. AND THE DIVERGENCE IS ALREADY RUNNING IN OUR ARITHMETIC: gossip() carries `inherited` and `of` so WHOSE deed it was survives every retelling, and forceOf applies GEN_LOSS 0.45 per generation, so the father fades on a fixed curve while the heir's own deeds enter at full weight and overtake him inside one life. NOTHING SHOWS IT HAPPENING. AND THE LAST STEP THROWS THE ATTRIBUTION AWAY: opinionOf returns a BARE NUMBER, dropping `of` and `inherited` after they survived the witness, the gossip and the fold. One field at one call site turns a number into a sentence. THE DELIVERABLE (what must CHANGE, not what carries) is section 6: who is in the room, what they assume, that it is about him, the weight shift, who vouches, the refusable trade, the taken seat -- and NOT the verbs and NOT the hands. MEASURED 9/5 by Q13 [child watches]: THE ROW'S PREMISE IS SLIGHTLY WRONG IN A USEFUL WAY. The learning research says WATCHING is the smaller half: people pick up a trade through LEGITIMATE PERIPHERAL PARTICIPATION, being allowed real but peripheral work inside a working community, and the authors are explicit that this is 'MORE THAN AN OBSERVATIONAL LOOKOUT POST'. The load-bearing word is LEGITIMATE. THE CHILD DOES NOT INHERIT WHAT THEY SAW, THEY INHERIT PERMISSION TO BE THERE. So the inheritance is a DOOR, not a stat -- and the games side agrees loudly (players call stat boosts between generations inelegant and a grind, and prefer unlocked options), which is day 9 horizontal and day 10 access-not-power reached independently for a third time. AND THE DOOR IS ALREADY BUILT: engine/bohemia_asking.js has SEVEN subjects (names, power, salvage, strangers, the hill, water, work), FOUR trades (keeper, scav, watch, worker), every subject answerable by TWO DIFFERENT TRADES, one refusal per trade, and it is referenced from the walked city. KNOWLEDGE IS ALREADY GATED BY WHICH TRADE WILL TALK TO YOU AND NOTHING CONNECTS THAT DOOR TO A PARENT. THE FINDING: our machine converts what you DID into what people SAY about you, and never into what your child is ALLOWED TO LEARN. THE HEIR INHERITS A REPUTATION AND NOT AN APPRENTICESHIP. The row's exact ask (what the heir starts with because of what the player DID, not what they owned) answers in one sentence: THE TRADES YOUR PARENT WORKED WITH ARE THE TRADES THAT WILL ANSWER YOU. NOT ANSWERED AND FLAGGED RATHER THAN PADDED: the row also asks 'at what age', and I found no defensible age figure and did not invent one. MEASURED 9/5 by Q14 [three arcs]: THERE ARE THREE GENERATIONS AND ONLY ONE VERB CHANGE, AND IT LANDS AT THE FIRST FOLD, NOT THE DRAMATIC ONE. The two folds are not the same kind of fold: at fold 1 the player has ~5 hours invested (round 1 measured eight animal verbs as a five-hour game) so losing it is CHEAP and is the strongest teaching device the game has; at fold 2 the player has ~90 hours and round 10 proved a new rule set makes a beginner of a master. THE FIRST FOLD CHANGES THE VERBS, THE SECOND MUST NOT. MEASURED IN THE DECODED FIGHT: our human verb list is EXACTLY NINE -- hit, move2, cover, kill, shot, open, quiet, dark, close -- each attached to a KIT ability with a need cost (PLATE UP, BREAK CONTACT, STEADY, SLIP, CALL IT, READ THE ROOM, PATCH IT, LIGHT IT, SEND HIM). SIDE BY SIDE, SEVEN OF THE ANIMAL'S EIGHT MAP ONTO A HUMAN VERB WE ALREADY BUILT, and the two gaps are the whole answer: the animal has SMELL and the human has nothing like it, and the human has OPEN and COVER which need hands and made things. THE FIRST FOLD IS A STRAIGHT TRADE: YOU LOSE THE NOSE AND YOU GAIN HANDS. Everything else survives it renamed, so it is teachable in ten minutes. AND GEN 1 IS NOT A SEPARATE GAME, IT IS OUR GAME WITH THE NOSE ON AND THE HANDS OFF, which is far smaller than 'design gen 1'. The real aisle points the same way: humans uniquely throw at high speed via elastic energy stored in the shoulder, the fastest motion the body makes, so the human signature is ACTING AT A DISTANCE WITH AN OBJECT (`shot`), the mirror of the animal knowing what happened here before it arrived. THE ANIMAL KNOWS THE PAST. THE HUMAN REACHES THE DISTANCE. THE ANGEL SEES THE WHOLE THING AND CAN TOUCH NONE OF IT. MEASURED 9/5 by Q15 [angel means]: OUR GAME CAN RECORD AND IT CANNOT RESOLVE. Searched engine/bohemia_standing.js, engine/bohemia_deeds.js and engine/bohemia_engine.js for forgive, forgiven, settle, settled, absolve, pardon, spare, redeem: ZERO HITS. The deeds module's whole function list is about getting a deed INTO the world (publish, publishStage, sayWhy, reachOf, hopsFor, cloutWeight, neutralWeight, scanQuest, loadCorpus) and there is no function that resolves one, so a deed is written, travels, fades and is never settled. THAT IS THE SAME HOLE FOUR ROUNDS OF THIS LANE FOUND FROM FOUR SIDES: Q4's write-only `wounds` (declared once, pushed once, never read), Q8's three-string monument, Q11's death that leaves a mark nobody can clear. AND WE ALREADY BUILT THE RECORDING ANGELS WITHOUT CALLING THEM THAT: the tradition gives every person TWO angels who write the good and the bad, and our engine gives every person TWO ledgers, `recordedKnown` (which the Amalgamation can model exactly) and `blindSpot` (which it can never see). Nothing anywhere compares the two for a person. THE ROW ASKED FOR VERBS AND ROUNDS 10 AND 16 SAID THERE ARE NONE, AND BOTH SURVIVE: the nine built verbs are things you do TO THE WORLD, and an intercessor's verbs are things you do ABOUT WHAT SOMEBODY ELSE DID, which is a different layer and not a tenth combat move, so the rules the player spent ninety hours learning are untouched. THE SIX: CARRY, WITNESS, WEIGH, SETTLE, SPARE, STAND BETWEEN -- five of which act on data we already write and never touch again. THE FIRST TWO GENERATIONS WRITE THE LEDGER. THE THIRD IS THE ONLY ONE THAT CAN CLOSE A LINE IN IT. Refused on purpose: any undo (SETTLE closes a line, it never erases a deed, because a deed that never happened breaks the witness web and Q11's rule that a scar is a repair and not a deletion), any right answer (the traditions themselves split on whether a guardian intercedes at all, so watching and intervening are separable and the choice is the mechanic), and a third refusal of an act-3 combat change. STATED HONESTLY: the cost argument (to stand between two things you have to stop being one of them) is MY reasoning from the role, not a citation -- I searched for what the role costs the one doing it and found nothing usable. Scope: engine searched, slices not swept. MEASURED 9/5 by Q16 [hundred hours]: WE ARE BUILDING THE HOUR AND THE GAME IS MADE OF RETURNS. 100 hours is 6,000 minutes; the mobile median session is 5 to 6 minutes (top quarter 8 to 9, about 4 sessions a day), so a hundred hours is between 1,000 returns at the median and 200 at a generous half-hour session. THAT IS THE MOST REPEATED MOMENT IN THE WHOLE GAME AND WE HAVE BUILT IT ZERO TIMES: swept every slice for a returning-player surface (welcome back, when you left, while you were gone, what you were doing) and got ZERO. AND THE RECAP IS ALREADY WRITTEN: 27 quests are baked into the alpha carrying 164 rows of kind:'journal', first person, past tense, one per stage, and THE ONLY CODE IN THE REPO THAT READS kind==='journal' IS THE DIRECTOR TOOL PAOLO TYPES THEM IN. The save protects the STATE perfectly and nothing anywhere protects the INTENT; a save that survives is not a player who returns. AND THE MIDDLE IS OUR THINNEST ACT, MEASURED THREE WAYS: boss ladder act1/act2/act3 = 20/12/7, laws numerals = 145/57/86, laws+records spelled out = 111/29/49. Act 2 loses every count, and the craft says the middle is exactly where long games die (three-act structure does not scale past 8 hours; the named failure is movement without progress). ALSO NOTHING KNOWS HOW LONG YOU HAVE PLAYED: zero hits for playtime, hoursPlayed, totalTime, minutesPlayed (control: 89 'hour' hits in the engine, all golden hour and wake times), so we claim a hundred hours and own no instrument that could ever tell us the middle sagged. COMPLETION IS THE COST OF LENGTH: about 14% finish primary content across games (one in seven), RPGs run ten points below that, a 61-hour campaign was finished by 15 to 17% while 16-hour games of other genres sit near 50%, so on the published curve MOST PEOPLE WHO START BOHEMIA NEVER MEET THE ANGEL and gen 3 is the least-seen content in the game. SAY THE GOOD PART TWICE: bohemia_save.js is the best-engineered thing this lane has measured (two slots with a generation counter so the newest good save is never the write target, FNV-1a checksum plus byte length verified on load, tombstones so a stale save can never resurrect, a probe sized to the real save, driven by a gate against a hostile fake browser), and RUN already shipped the home screen answer to WebKit's seven-day storage wipe, which is the hardest constraint a hundred-hour phone game has. AND THE MIDDLE HORIZON IS BUILT AND CAPPED: the mandate ladder is inlined on the walked surface behind a real STANDING button reading live standings, three rungs for three acts, and MAYOR_SHARE is STILL null at slices/BOHEMIA_CITY_WORLD.html:11031 (verified live this round, not quoted from the 8/28 law), so it tops out at two rungs of three while the evidence says proximal week-sized goals are the only goal distance with a demonstrated effect and distal goals have none. A PENDING SINCE 6/30 IS SITTING ON THE ONLY MOTIVATION STRUCTURE THE RESEARCH SUPPORTS. AND THE MIDDLE SAG IS THE SAME BUG AS ROUND 9 AND ROUND 17: a middle cannot escalate in a world with no subtraction (no -= and no Math.min in the fold) and cannot conclude in a game where no deed can be settled. Also the spine is short: the live ladder is v7 with 53 candidates against the 60-boss pillar and its own header calls it a pool to cut from. Reference law obeyed: BATTLE BROTHERS only (this row IS the campaign layer), and the completion research's game titles were STRIPPED so no unnamed reference enters through the back door. Scope: engine AND slices both swept this round. ALSO: nothing replaces or ages out the population at a generation boundary (control: 'cohort' appears once in the engine and it is the comment REFUSING a birth-year generator), so 100% of the cast survives our fold against a measured real figure near 3% after ten years. THE FINDING: a time skip in games is an art job, in life it is a CAST CHANGE -- the room stays and the people go -- and round 4 already showed our districts can only get NICER, so we cannot do the art half and should not want to. AND TEN YEARS COLD CLAUSE 1 ALREADY RULES THE CUT: do not play the ten years, the same way the crash is never played.
CORRECTED 9/7 (Paolo): Animal / Human / Angel are the three ERAS (the anarchy decade, the world clawing back, the cyberpunk healing), never creatures. Rounds 1 and 2 researched a literal animal on a misreading; they stay as records, their coyote findings route to wildlife, and "what you can do with no hands" is void for the player. The lane's subject is unchanged otherwise: the fold, the heir, ageing, what carries.

## EYES AND EARS  (17. his second pair of eyes and ears. Checks that every shipped visual and sound is correct and not weak. Never decides taste; that is DIRECTION.)
MODE: SCHOOL THEN CHECK (Paolo 9/6, LOCKED: "eyes and ears gotta still be going to school bro, rounds of big brain research. Maybe each request for eyes and ears becomes a round of online research for that topic before it does it, so maybe eyes and ears takes 2 rounds"). *** EVERY JOB IN THIS LANE IS TWO ROUNDS AND NEVER ONE. ROUND ONE IS SCHOOL: before you measure anything, spend a whole VAMILY researching that job's topic online -- how the best games in the world do it, what the real craft or science says, what the standard instrument is and what it gets wrong -- and write it as a record. NO MEASURING IN ROUND ONE. ROUND TWO IS THE CHECK: build the instrument and run it, armed by what you learned, and say in the record which finding from round one changed how you measured. A job marked SHIPPED without both rounds is not shipped. If a job looks easy enough to do in one round, that is the sign you have not learned enough about it yet. *** This lane MAY build its own checking instruments (screenshot passes, audio measurement, tools/ and gates/ that only check); it nevererer writes game code. Paolo 9/4 asked how long until it is worth having: useful for visuals after E3, for sound after E4, decent after about eight rounds, so the queue is ordered fastest-to-useful.
STATE: nothing exists. No screenshot pass, no golden images, no audio measurement, no glitch checklist. Today the only pair of eyes and ears on shipped art and sound is Paolo. The 9/4 compare law and the runway law give this lane its bar; the 7/18 VERIFY ON THE REAL SURFACE law is its religion.

## PLUMBER  (18. THE PERMANENT PIPE FIXER. Paolo 9/5: "we can have a permanent pipe fixer always working... helping make the game as slimmest it can be, fastest it can be, 60 fps." That is the job, in three parts: (1) the game runs at 60 on a real phone, loads fast and stays small; (2) every rule has a working checker (about 230 small test programs, called gates) and the whole set finishes in one command in under ten minutes; (3) the two pile files that eat everyone's tokens get archived. It never builds features, never touches art, never cleans other chats' game code for style. It measures, sets a budget, and builds the checker that holds the budget, so the other chats find out the moment they break it.)
MODE: BUILD
STATE: ~230 gates; the full suite cannot finish in its time budget (LAB, 8/31); 82 gates never ran last attempt; gate colours are per-lane guesses. Frame rate, load time and size on a real phone have NEVER been measured (dispatch item 7, unowned since 8/25). The alpha is ~11 MB of HTML plus a base64 fight; the demo is 4.6 MB. A law without a machine gate is not enforced, so this chat is what makes every other rule real.
WHAT THIS CHAT MAY TOUCH: gates/, the build/cut tooling, and the two pile files it is told to archive (the handoff and the backlog), verbatim moves only. It may also shrink or speed up ENGINE code, but only where a measurement says so, only where no gate changes colour, and it writes the number before and after in the commit. WHAT IT MAY NOT TOUCH: slices/ content, laws/, any art, any feature. A red gate it finds in another lane's work becomes ONE line in that lane's section, never a fix by this chat.

Measured: one boot is 8.0 s, fourteen boots are 112 s, and RUN ALONE WITH THE CAP LIFTED THE GATE IS GREEN -- 102 passed, 0 failed, in 1,228 s (20.5 min). So it is not a slow failing gate, it is 102 WORKING CHECKS ON THE FACTION SYSTEM THAT THE SUITE HAS NEVER ONCE BEEN ALLOWED TO SEE, filed under the 93 reds the whole time. Worse than green over nothing: a RED over 102 real greens. Boots are 9%; ONE SECTION IS 48% (K, WHAT ASKING COSTS, 594 s for 3 checks), so splitting the gate means splitting K and K alone is already near the cap. Reading the source told me where the time went and was wrong by five times: time is measured, never read. 12 s of declared waiting. AND THE RATCHET FIRED ON ANOTHER LANE WITHIN HOURS: COOK took two files off the frozen list on [hair colours] and wrote down why -- both slices INLINE THE ENGINE, so the +938/-55 was TWO WEEKS OF EVERY LANE'S ENGINE WORK unrebuilt, not one careless bake. ROUND 2: THE 594 s SECTION IS NOW 357 s AND EVERY ONE OF THE 102 CHECK LINES IS WORD-FOR-WORD IDENTICAL. Measured inside it rather than read: the ring walks 14 bases x 12 offsets and the rings OVERLAP, so it made 5,112 passes over 441 DISTINCT PEOPLE -- 4,671 of them (91%) re-opening a UI card on somebody whose outfit had already been read, at 116 ms a pass. Skipping an already-answered person is result-identical because `seen` only records a fid the FIRST time, so every later pass already fell through and changed nothing; a NULL read is still retried, because a card that failed once may fill elsewhere and losing that would lose an outfit. - SHIPPED 9/7 plumber-ont6t5  [clock math]  HOW-MANY-GAME-MINUTES-PER-REAL-MINUTE -- MEASURED, PUBLISHED AND GATED. Four runs on the demo, walking the real city with a real held thumb and reading the game's own clock either side: 16.28 / 16.38 / 16.49 / 16.53 IN-GAME MINUTES PER REAL MINUTE walking, and ZERO idle, four out of four. SO A WAKING DAY (960 in-game minutes, 06:00 to nightfall) TAKES 58 TO 59 REAL MINUTES, A HUNDRED HOURS HOLDS 102 DAYS, and a five-minute session is 8% of a day. The craft runs a day in about 14 real minutes, so ours is FOUR TIMES LONGER -- and the research's two guesses were 15 minutes (400 days) or 90 minutes (66 days, three generations impossible); the truth is between them, 34 lived days per generation. THE INSTRUMENT WAS WRONG FIRST BY 3.5x, and how it was caught is worth keeping: holding one direction walks you into a wall and then stands there, and TWO RUNS BOTH STOPPED AT EXACTLY 28 CELLS, which is not a walking speed, it is the distance to the nearest obstacle. gates/clock_rate_gate.js (9/0 through the suite) holds that standing still earns nothing, that the measured minutes-per-cell agrees with the 0.084 the day loop declares (so the 8/19 bug where walking could not move the clock at all cannot come back quietly), and a wide tripwire band; THE DAY LENGTH ITSELF IS PRINTED AND NEVER ASSERTED because it is a DIAL and it is RUN's ([a day is]). records/BOHEMIA_A_DAY_TAKES_FIFTY_EIGHT_REAL_MINUTES_9_7_26.md -- from this round's swing (records/BOHEMIA_COORDINATOR_RESEARCH_HOW_LONG_IS_A_DAY_9_7_26.md): a day is 1,440 in-game minutes, a step is 0.084 of them, standing still costs nothing, and NOBODY has measured how many in-game minutes pass per real minute of play. THE TARGET IS 400 AND IT IS NOT MET. WHAT IS LEFT, ALL OF IT SOMEBODY ELSE'S: (a) the cover camera eases 10% a frame toward a target set by how far the enemies are, so it is almost never still and the floor cache is a lottery worth between nothing and 150 ms -- a camera that SNAPPED when within a fraction of a pixel would fix it and that is how the camera FEELS, so COMBAT's; (b) the canvas is fully painted at least three times a frame before a character -- cutting that changes the picture, so DIRECTION and COMBAT; (c) a real handset, same [PENDING Paolo] as [sixty fps]. AND A WARNING FOR WHOEVER TAKES IT: the driven beat has a 40 ms NOISE FLOOR between identical runs, so a single before-and-after is a coin toss; use alternating pairs inside one boot. records/BOHEMIA_NEITHER_THE_CALLS_NOR_THE_PIXELS_9_7_26.md and records/BOHEMIA_A_FIGHT_NOBODY_IS_PLAYING_9_7_26.md) -- this lane's own profile found it and it now reorders the board (records/BOHEMIA_COORDINATOR_THE_FIGHT_HAS_NO_HEADROOM_9_6_26.md): a fighting beat spends 497 ms of its 500, 99.5% of the main thread, and two thirds of that is ONE call, drawImage. Plus 15 ms of EVERY beat is spent animating a hidden zero-by-zero panel at 60 fps before any fight has happened. Take the free 15 ms first, then the five things the craft says actually fix a drawImage wall: the cost is per CALL not per pixel, so cut call COUNT; pre-render repeated and static things once to an offscreen canvas and blit them whole; layer the canvases so what never moves is not cleared every frame; never scale inside drawImage, cache the sizes at load; round x and y to whole numbers. TARGET: a fighting beat under 400 ms of 500 on the desktop harness, then measured on a real phone. Top of this lane.
